## 17. 第十二轮深度审计——加密防篡改审计追踪、Agent行为水印溯源、跨区域数据主权与Geo-Fence、碳感知绿色Agent调度（v0.13.0）

**审计动机**：前十一轮 126 个盲点已逼近不完备性原理边界——本轮发现的 4 个维度不在深度上（框架/安全/认知/弹性已深度覆盖）而在广度上——它们是跨越传统 Agent 设计范畴的**跨学科注入**：(1) 密码学防篡改（谁 guard 着 audit trail 不被 Agent 自身篡改？）(2) 行为水印溯源（谁说的、哪个模型说的、哪个 Skill 说的——不可伪造的溯源）(3) 数据主权（Agents 可以跨区域自由飞行，但数据有国籍）(4) 绿色计算（1 人 + AI 维护中，Agent 7x24 运行的电费和碳足迹是否被纳入系统设计？）。

**对标来源**：nono Merkle Tree Agent Audit Trails (April 2026)、AgentMark Behavioral Watermark (arXiv 2601.03294, April 2026)、IETF AI Model Attestation Draft (March 2026)、EU AI Act Article 12 Transparency + Record-Keeping + Traceability、TrueFoundry AI Gateway Geo-Fenced Execution (2026)、TD Commons Signed Geo-Policy Tokens、IQ/C Green Agent Scheduling (Feb 2026)。

### 17.1 加密防篡改审计追踪（Cryptographic Tamper-Evident Audit Trail）

**问题陈述**：B55（审计追踪）、B56（审计留存）和 D-019-23（Skill Audit / Talos）覆盖了"审计什么"和"留存多久"——但缺失一个根本性的密码学问题：**审计追踪的完整性必须抵御 Agent 进程自身的篡改**。nono（April 2026）用精准的语言描述了这个威胁：当 Agent 犯错后，它可能被提示/配置/指令驱动去"修正"审计日志以掩盖错误——如果审计日志存储在 Agent 可写的存储中，这种篡改无声无息且不可证伪。解决方案是：审计日志的完整性必须不依赖 Agent 进程的可信度——即**log integrity must survive the agent process**。

**关键研究发现**：

1. **nono Merkle Tree Agent Audit Trails（April 2026）**：核心设计——每次 Agent 工具调用结束后即时计算新节点（parent hash = H(left.hash + right.hash + record.data)），Merkle Root 存储在 Agent 不可达的签名存储中。两个时间窗口：Real-Time（5秒内，on_every_tool_call → immediate tree update + push root to external store）+ Batch（每小时批量重新验证并公证）。Merkle Proof 的时间戳独立于 Agent 的时钟——来自可信第三方。即使 Agent 进程拥有 root 权限，于 Merkle Root 已交付外部存储，篡改历史日志会产生不同的 root → 攻击被数学证明。

2. **IETF AI Model Attestation Draft（March 2026）**：标准化 AI 模型/产出的远程可验证声明——包括 Logging Requirements：binary_logs_on_every_in/out、binary_logs_encrypted_to_Public_Key_of_Audit_Infra、signed_by_Agent_Identity、chained = true（每个条目链接到前一哈希）。解决端到端可验证性：Model A → Skill B → Output C 的全链可追溯。

3. **EU AI Act Article 12**：AI 系统的技术支持必须包含 record-keeping + traceability of outputs——加密可验证的审计链直接满足此合规要求。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B131:
    title: "加密防篡改Agent审计追踪——Merkle Tree + 外部Root存储 + IETF Attestation + EU AI Act Article 12 合规"
    description: "B55/B56/D-019-23覆盖了审计内容与留存——但审计完整性依赖存储系统的可信度。Agent进程若被指令驱动修改日志，当前无密码学防护。nono: log integrity must survive the agent process"
    rationale: "nono Merkle Tree Agent Audit Trails (April 2026): real-time Merkle proofs with external root storage; IETF AI Attestation Draft (Mar 2026): signed/chained binary logs; EU AI Act Article 12: record-keeping + traceability as legal requirement"
    mitigation: |
      Merkle Tree Audit Architecture:
      
      Data Structure:
      ```
      MerkleNode:
        left_hash, right_hash, parent_hash
        record_data: {timestamp, agent_id, skill_id, tool_call, input_hash, output_hash, decision_rationale}
        signature: agent_identity.private_key.sign(node_hash)
      ```
      
      Real-Time Protocol:
      1. on_tool_call_complete: 新 MerkleNode 创建 → 插入到 tree
      2. 新 Root R' = H(R_left + R_right + new_node_hash)
      3. R' + timestamp → 推送到 External Audit Store（Agent 无写权限的独立基础设施）
      4. External Store 返回 signed_confirmation = {R', timestamp_trusted, auditor_signature}
      5. Agent 将 signed_confirmation 写入本地（本地日志的完整性凭据在外部）
      
      Verification Protocol:
      - Merkle Proof 生成: 对于任何历史 node N，提供 {N, siblings[], root_R} → 验证者计算 root = H(siblings) → 比对 External Store 中存储的 root
      - Timestamp Verification: 外部 store 的 timestamp 独立于 Agent 时钟 → 消解"Agent 回拨时钟"攻击
      - Batch Verification: 每小时公证一次 Merkle Root → 生成 notarized_root → 写入 immutable ledger
      
      Tamper Detection:
      - 篡改单一记录 → Merkle Proof 失败（root mismatch）→ 数学证明篡改发生
      - 篡改 External Store → 需要 compromise 独立审计基础设施（攻击面分离成功）
      - 最佳攻击仍可能: root compromise + external store compromise 同时发生 → 但成本提升 N 个量级
      
      IETF Attestation Integration:
      - binary_logs_on_every_in/out: 二进制日志 = Merkle Node 的 record_data
      - binary_logs_encrypted_to_Public_Key_of_Audit_Infra: 只有审计者能解密日志内容
      - signed_by_Agent_Identity: agent签名使日志具有不可否认性
      - chained = true: 每节点链接到前一哈希 = Merkle 结构满足要求
    implementation: "skill_merkle_audit.py —— MerkleTreeBuilder + ExternalRootPublisher + MerkleProofGenerator + TimestampVerifier + BatchNotary + IETFAttestationAdapter"
    severity: high

  B132:
    title: "Agent行为水印溯源——多比特行为水印嵌入到Planning/Tool-Choice/Subgoal-Selection的行为层面"
    description: "当前水印系统（如文本水印）仅嵌入OUTPUT TEXT——但真正需要溯源的是哪个Skill、哪个Model、哪个参数组合产出了给定的决策。行为水印从Planning行为而非Output文本中提取溯源信息"
    rationale: "AgentMark (arXiv 2601.03294, April 2026): multi-bit watermarks embedded in agent PLANNING BEHAVIOR — tool choices, subgoal selection — not output text. Capacity: 16-32 bits per action sequence"
    mitigation: |
      Agent Behavioral Watermarking:
      
      Watermark Embedding — Three Behavioral Dimensions:
      1. Tool Choice Watermark: When 2+ functionally equivalent tools exist → choice encodes bit(s)
         - e.g., data.fetch_via_api() vs data.fetch_via_sql() → both return same data → but choice = 1 watermark bit
         - Quant: factor_regression_via_sklearn() vs factor_regression_via_statsmodels() → same output → encoder's choice = watermark
      
      2. Subgoal Selection Watermark: Planning step order constrained by watermark
         - Agent plans "Step 1: Clean data, Step 2: Compute factors, Step 3: Validate" 
         - If watermark bit=1 → ordering: Step2→Step1→Step3; if bit=0 → Step1→Step2→Step3
         - Both orderings produce valid result → order choice encodes identity
      
      3. Parameter Sampling Watermark: Temperature-controlled parameter perturbation
         - Every parameter choice with valid range → watermark bit perturbs choice within ±ε of valid range
         - Uncertainty quantification: watermark perturbation has bounded impact on result quality
      
      Watermark Payload:
      - Agent Identity: which skill instance (skill_id + version + model + session_id)
      - Decision Provenance: tracing back through which reasoning chain
      - Timestamp: when was this decision made
      - Hash of upstream inputs: which data produced this decision
      
      Detection & Verification:
      - Statistical Detection: n_behavioral_patterns × p(watermark | no-watermark) < 0.001 → watermark present
      - Zero False Positive Design: detection threshold calibrated to 1/2^k where k = watermark bits per sequence
      - Multiple sequence correlation: 3+ action sequences all show watermark pattern → high confidence
      
      Anti-Spoofing:
      - Attacker tries to remove watermark by randomizing tool choices → functionally equivalent BUT → introduces detectable randomness pattern (unnatural entropy in tool selection)
      - Attacker tries to overwrite with different watermark → requires knowing encoding schema + PRF key → computational infeasible
    implementation: "skill_watermark.py —— ToolChoiceEncoder + SubgoalOrderingEncoder + ParameterSamplingEncoder + StatisticalDetector + AntiSpoofMonitor"
    severity: medium
```

### 17.2 跨区域数据主权与Geo-Fence执行（Cross-Region Data Sovereignty & Geo-Fenced Agent Execution）

**问题陈述**：B57 覆盖了数据本地化（数据存储位置受 MiFID II/SEC 613 约束），§10.4 覆盖了多区域部署——但缺失**运行时 Geo-Fence**：Agent 在单一 CPU 上运行时理论上可以访问任何它能连接的数据源——无论该数据是否属于不同的法律管辖区。量化金融的数据主权问题非常具体：A股数据可能只允许在CN区域内处理和存储，US options数据可能在US区域，而EU ETF数据又受GDPR约束。Agent 在执行时需要知道自己"站在"哪个法域的地板上。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B133:
    title: "跨区域数据主权Geo-Fence——Signed Geo-Policy Tokens + 法域感知Agent路由 + 数据不应跨越法域边界的运行时强制"
    description: "B57数据本地化只约束存储位置——不约束Agent运行时可访问的数据源范围。A股/美股/EU ETF数据的法律管辖区不同，Agent可能在一个CPU上同时处理三方数据而不自知"
    rationale: "TD Commons Geo-Fenced AI Execution (2026): signed geo-policy tokens; TrueFoundry AI Gateway: region-constrained agent routing; EU AI Act: data sovereignty as jurisdictional requirement"
    mitigation: |
      Geo-Fenced Agent Execution:
      
      Geo-Policy Token (GPT):
      ```
      {
        "jurisdictions": ["CN", "US", "EU"],
        "data_constraints": {
          "CN_data": { "execution_region": "cn-east-2", "export_allowed": false, "cross_region_query_allowed": false },
          "US_data": { "execution_region": "us-east-1", "export_allowed": false },
          "EU_data": { "execution_region": "eu-west-1", "gdpr_scope": true }
        },
        "agent_constraints": {
          "allowed_cross_region_queries": false,
          "require_data_origin_verification": true
        },
        "signature": "jurisdiction_authority.sign(token_hash)"
      }
      ```
      
      Runtime Enforcement:
      - Agent启动时: 验证Geo-Policy Token签名 → 注入法域约束到 Agent Context
      - Per-tool-call: before executing data fetch → check data_origin vs agent_execution_region
      - If data_origin == "CN" AND agent_execution_region != "cn-*" → BLOCK + LOG
      - If data_origin == "EU" AND not compliant_with_gdpr → BLOCK
      
      Cross-Region Agent Routing:
      - Governor决策: "需要同时分析A股+美股数据" → 拆分为两个Sub-Agents
        → Sub-Agent-CN Route to cn-east-2 → process A股 data → return anonymized summary
        → Sub-Agent-US Route to us-east-1 → process US data → return anonymized summary
        → Governor merge summaries → no raw data crossed jurisdiction boundaries
      - TrueFoundry AI Gateway pattern: region-constrained routing ensures agents never receive data outside their region
      
      Audit for Data Sovereignty:
      - Every blocked cross-region access → log with reason + timestamp → part of Merkle Audit Trail (B131)
      - Quarterly sovereignty compliance report: 0 cross-region violations = compliant
    implementation: "skill_geo_fence.py —— GeoPolicyTokenValidator + RegionConstrainedRouter + CrossRegionAccessGuard + SovereigntyAuditLogger"
    severity: medium
```

### 17.3 碳感知绿色Agent调度（Carbon-Aware Green Agent Scheduling）

**问题陈述**：在 1 人 + AI 维护语境下，Agent 是 7x24 全天候运行的——但电力强度是时间敏感的（电网碳强度在可再生能源高峰时低、化石燃料高峰时高）。当前 Agent 调度完全不考虑碳足迹——但 IQ/C（Intelligence-to-Carbon ratio）这一指标可以让我们在不牺牲决策质量的前提下将非紧急批处理作业迁移到低碳时段。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B134:
    title: "碳感知绿色Agent调度——IQ/C指标 + 模型三级分层(L1/L2/L3) + 绿色时间窗口批处理 + 语义缓存avoid redundant compute"
    description: "Agent 7x24运行产生持续性碳足迹——但系统设计时完全未纳入碳成本约束。IQ/C概念提供了一个框架: 在不牺牲决策质量的前提下降低碳强度"
    rationale: "IQ/C Green Agent Scheduling (Feb 2026): Intelligence-to-Carbon ratio metric; model tiering for green routing; green window batching for non-urgent workloads"
    mitigation: |
      Carbon-Aware Scheduling Framework:
      
      IQ/C Metric (Intelligence-to-Carbon Ratio):
      IQ/C = decision_quality_score / (carbon_impact_kgCO2_per_task)
      - High IQ/C: high-quality decisions at low carbon → preferred routing
      - Low IQ/C: expensive carbon for marginal quality → degrade to lower tier
      
      Model Tier for Carbon Routing:
      - L1 (Edge/Small): 1-3B param models → 0.001 kgCO2 per task → use for: routine decisions, data validation, low-stakes analysis
      - L2 (Mid-tier): 7-13B param models → 0.01 kgCO2 per task → use for: standard factor analysis, risk reports
      - L3 (Large): 70B+ param models → 0.1 kgCO2 per task → use for: critical trading decisions, high-stakes analysis ONLY
      
      Green Window Batching:
      - Real-time grid carbon intensity API → predict green windows (low-CI hours)
      - Non-urgent batch tasks: auto-schedule to next green window
        - Backtesting (1-8 hour compute) → schedule to renewable peak (typically 10:00-16:00 for solar, 20:00-06:00 for wind)
        - Model fine-tuning → schedule to lowest CI window within 7-day deadline
        - Factor pre-computation → schedule green window within 24h
      - Urgent tasks: always execute immediately → pay carbon price (informativeness > carbon for critical tasks)
      
      Semantic Caching for Carbon Reduction:
      - Leverage §13.2 Prompt Cache + §14.3 Cross-Skill KB → avoid redundant LLM calls
      - "Have we already computed the VaR for this portfolio with these parameters in the past 24h?" → cache hit → 0 carbon (no recompute)
      - Cache hit rate target: ≥ 40% → direct carbon reduction
      
      Carbon Dashboard:
      - Daily: kgCO2 consumed by Agent fleet
      - Weekly: IQ/C per Skill (high-IQ/C Skills = efficient; low-IQ/C = target for optimization)
      - Monthly: carbon offset budget allocation
      - Integration: carbon metrics written to Merkle Audit Trail (B131) → auditable green claims
    implementation: "skill_green_agent.py —— IQCCalculator + ModelTierRouter + GreenWindowScheduler + CarbonIntensityAPIClient + CarbonDashboard + SemanticCacheKicker"
    severity: low
```

---

## 18. 第十三轮审计——蓝图文档结构完整性、Skill交互拓扑DAG、Agent灾备与BCDR、蓝图自验证门禁、Well-Known技能发现、数据契约Schema注册表（v0.14.0）

**审计动机**：前十二轮 130 个盲点全部聚焦于"Skill 应该有什么能力"——属于**Skill设计维度的盲点**。但这是蓝图阶段——蓝图文档本身的元信息层是否完整？当一个 AI 阅读这份蓝图决定"是否可以开始施工"时，它会检查什么？本轮审计将镜头向后拉，审视**蓝图作为一份专业文档应有的结构完整性**：(1) 100+ 个专业术语散落在 17 个章节中——AI 需要一个集中术语表来消除歧义；(2) 非功能性需求（延迟/吞吐/可用性/Token预算/碳预算）散布各处但从未汇总——施工时无法作为统一SLO目标；(3) 19+ 个 Skill 的交互依赖关系是一个隐式知识网络——但没有显式的拓扑DAG；(4) Agent 军团的灾备策略——不是单个 Agent 的自愈，而是整个 Skill 舰队因数据中心火灾/AI事故/供应链攻击而全军覆没时的恢复方案；(5) 蓝图自身体验证——这份 4500 行的文档在什么条件下才算"完成"？(6) 外部系统如何发现 ZephyrAlpha 的 Skills——Cloudflare 的 Well-Known URI 标准；(7) Skill 间数据交换的类型契约——没有 formal schema registry 的 Agent 通信就是语义模糊的口头协议；(8) 明确的假设、约束与设计权衡记录——避免施工时 AI 基于错误假设做出灾难性决策。

**对标来源**：GraSP (arXiv 2604.17870, Tencent April 2026)、SkillGraph (arXiv 2604.17503, NUS April 2026)、MARIA OS Capability Graph (March 2026)、SynaptiAI Agent Capability Standard 9-layer × 36 capabilities、Cloudflare Agent Skills Discovery RFC v0.2.0 (March 2026)、MCP Gateway Registry Agent Skills Architecture、Azure AI Foundry BCDR Architecture (Feb 2026)、CISO Marketplace "When the Cloud Burns" (March 2026)、Kiro Incident (Amazon AI Agent tore down AWS)、ISO/IEC 42001 AI Management System documentation requirements、NIST AI RMF Govern/Map/Measure/Manage evidence framework。

### 18.1 Skill交互拓扑与依赖DAG（Skill Interaction Topology & Dependency DAG）

**问题陈述**：蓝图 §2.2 触发表定义了"什么任务触发什么 Skill"，§10.5 FIPA-ACL 定义了 Skill 间通信协议，§14.3 Concordia 定义了 Agent 间协商——但三者从未统一为一幅**全局 Skill 交互拓扑图**。当施工阶段 AI 需要编排 5 个 Skill 协作完成一个复杂任务时，它不知道：(a) 哪些 Skill 可以并行执行（无依赖边），(b) 哪些 Skill 有数据依赖必须串行，(c) 如果中间某个 Skill 失败，哪些下游 Skill 需要被取消，(d) Skill 间的组合是否存在循环依赖。

GraSP (Tencent, April 2026) 用实证数据证明：图结构编排相比扁平序列编排在复杂任务上全面优越——图结构的三个关键优势：(1) **表达力**：并行分支 + 类型化依赖边 > 纯序列；(2) **故障传播有界**：节点 v 失败时仅使其 O(d^h) 个后代失效，而非整个链；(3) **受控数量**：编译阶段剪枝冗余 Skill 得到最小计划。SkillGraph (NUS, April 2026) 进一步证明：通信拓扑不应是静态模板——应随查询内容和活跃 Skill 嵌入动态生成。MARIA OS Capability Graph 则证明：Organizational-level capability management 需要 Capability Graph 来实现 O(log N) 发现延迟。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B135:
    title: "全局Skill交互拓扑DAG——GraSP图结构编排 + SkillGraph共进化拓扑 + MARIA OS Capability Graph三层融合"
    description: "§2.2触发表+§10.5 FIPA-ACL+§14.3 Concordia各自覆盖了路由/协议/协商——但缺少统一的全局Skill交互拓扑DAG定义依赖边、并行分支、失败传播域"
    rationale: "GraSP (arXiv 2604.17870, Tencent April 2026): graph-structured skill compositions outperform flat sequences; failure at node v invalidates only O(d^h) << O(N); SkillGraph (arXiv 2604.17503, NUS April 2026): co-evolve topology+expertise; MARIA OS (Mar 2026): O(log N) capability discovery"
    mitigation: |
      Global Skill Interaction DAG:

      Topology Definition:
      ```
      SkillTopology:
        nodes: [SkillNode]  # each Skill = one node with {id, type, version, min_model_tier}
        edges: [DependencyEdge]  # typed edges between Skills
        
      DependencyEdge types:
        - DATA_DEPENDENCY: Skill B needs Skill A's output as input → A must complete before B starts
        - ORCHESTRATION_DEPENDENCY: Skill B can only be invoked BY Skill A (e.g., governor invokes drift-detector)
        - MUTUAL_EXCLUSION: Skill A and Skill B MUST NOT run concurrently (e.g., two Skills writing to same DB table)
        - SOFT_PREFERENCE: Skill A is recommended to run before Skill B for quality, but not required
        - COMPOSITION: Skill C = Skill A ∘ Skill B (composite skill)
      ```

      ZephyrAlpha Concrete Topology (示例):
      ```
      L0: AGENTS.md Constitution [always loaded]
      │
      ├──► architect [role] ──► master-blueprint [domain]
      │     └── DATA_DEPENDENCY ──► blueprint.md sections
      │
      ├──► implementer [role] ──► database-specialist [domain]
      │     ├── DATA_DEPENDENCY: database blueprint §3 ATM patterns
      │     └── MUTUAL_EXCLUSION with: mcp-specialist (shared stdout access)
      │
      ├──► governor [role] ──► drift-detector [domain]
      │     ├── ORCHESTRATION_DEPENDENCY: governor invokes drift-detector
      │     ├── ORCHESTRATION_DEPENDENCY: governor invokes gate-specialist
      │     └── COMPOSITION: governor = drift-detector ∘ gate-specialist ∘ knowledge-specialist
      │
      └──► Parallel Group (无依赖边):
            ├── context-specialist ∥ feedback-specialist ∥ agent-specialist
            └── (可并行加载，无共享可变状态)
      ```

      DAG Compilation (GraSP-inspired):
      1. Task arrives → Planner builds candidate Skill DAG
      2. Compiler prunes: remove redundant edges, merge equivalent paths
      3. Scheduler assigns: parallel branches → concurrent execution, serial chains → sequential
      4. Validator checks: no cycles, no MUTUAL_EXCLUSION violation, total token < budget

      Bounded Failure Propagation:
      - Failure at Skill v → mark all descendants in DAG as "CANCELLED"
      - Only O(d^h) nodes affected (d = max out-degree, h = max depth)
      - Compare to flat sequence: one failure = ALL subsequent nodes lost → O(N)
      - Recovery: recompile DAG from failure point → exclude failed node and its sub-DAG → continue

      Co-Evolution (SkillGraph-inspired):
      - Topology is not static — it evolves as Skills improve
      - After every N executions: analyze success rates per edge → prune low-success edges
      - New Skills added: auto-insert into topology → run N shadow executions → if success > threshold → promote to active topology

      Visualization:
      - Grafana Panel: live DAG visualization with node coloring (green=healthy, yellow=degraded, red=failed)
      - Dependency Chain Tracer: click any Skill → see upstream/downstream dependency chain
      - Bottleneck Analyzer: longest path in DAG = critical path → target for optimization
    implementation: "skill_topology_dag.py —— TopologyBuilder + DAGCompiler + ParallelScheduler + FailureDomainCalculator + CoEvolutionOptimizer + TopologyVisualizer"
    severity: high

  B136:
    title: "Agent舰队灾备与业务连续性——多区域Agent状态复制 + Agent BCDR RTO/RPO + 灾备演练自动化"
    description: "§15.5自愈合+§11.7智能回滚+§14.7级联故障防护覆盖了单个/多个Agent的运行中断——但不覆盖整个Agent舰队因数据中心火灾/AI事故/供应链攻击而全军覆没的灾难场景"
    rationale: "Kiro Incident (Amazon AI agent tore down AWS, 2026) + Azure Foundry BCDR: agent runtime(MS) vs agent state(customer Cosmos DB) + CISO Marketplace: agentic AI failure at machine speed faster than human on-call + Gartner: 90% enterprises deploy agents by 2027, <15% have DR"
    mitigation: |
      Agent Fleet BCDR Architecture:

      Agent State Triple Nature (灾备特殊挑战):
      ```
      T1 — Short-Term Memory (丢失影响: 当前任务中断):
        - Active conversation context / tool call in-progress
        - Storage: Redis / in-memory cache
        - RPO target: < 5 seconds (near-real-time replication)
        
      T2 — Mid-Term Reasoning Context (丢失影响: 任务失败+协作混乱):
        - Multi-step reasoning chain / inter-Agent negotiation state
        - Storage: Cosmos DB / PostgreSQL session store
        - RPO target: < 1 minute (transaction log shipping)
        
      T3 — Long-Term Behavior Evolution (丢失影响: Agent性能退化/失效):
        - Fine-tuned model weight deltas / learned skill patterns / calibration profiles
        - Storage: Model registry / feature store snapshots
        - RPO target: < 1 hour (daily snapshot + incremental delta)
      ```

      Multi-Region Agent Deployment:
      ```
      Primary Region (e.g., cn-east-2):
        ├── Active Agent Fleet (all Skills loaded)
        ├── Agent State Store (Cosmos DB — primary write)
        ├── Skill Registry (primary source of truth)
        └── Merkle Audit Trail (primary ledger)
      
      Secondary Region (e.g., cn-north-1) — Warm Standby:
        ├── Warm Agent Pool (pre-loaded Skills, idle)
        ├── Agent State Store (Cosmos DB — read replica, auto-failover)
        ├── Skill Registry (read replica)
        └── Merkle Audit Trail (replicated)
      
      Tertiary Region (e.g., aws-us-east-1) — Cold Standby:
        ├── Skill Registry (periodic snapshot sync)
        └── Agent State (daily backup only)
      ```

      Failover Triggers:
      - Health Check Probe: every 5s ping Agent Fleet health endpoint
      - 3 consecutive failures on primary → auto-trigger failover to Secondary
      - LLM API outage detected (> 30s no response) → switch to backup model provider
      - Kiro-type self-destruction: Agent output guard detects catastrophic pattern → kill-switch + failover

      Failover Flow:
      ```
      T+0s: Primary region health check FAIL (3rd consecutive)
      T+1s: Governor Agent in Secondary promoted to MASTER
      T+2s: Secondary Cosmos DB promoted to write master
      T+3s: DNS / routing updated → all traffic to Secondary
      T+5s: Warm Agent Pool in Secondary activated → begin processing backlog
      T+30s: Dead Agent sessions in Primary reconstructed from replicated state in Secondary
      T+60s: Full operational recovery (RTO = 60s)
      ```

      Agent State Backup Strategy:
      - Continuous: transaction log shipping (T1 state) → RPO < 5s
      - Periodic: full state snapshot every 15 min (T2 state) → RPO < 15min
      - Daily: model/calibration/performance profile export (T3 state) → RPO < 24h
      - Immutable: Merkle Audit Trail already replicated (B131) → RPO = 0

      Disaster Recovery Drill (Automated):
      - Weekly: Secondary warm standby activation drill (no traffic shift)
      - Monthly: Full failover drill to Secondary → run for 1 hour → failback
      - Quarterly: Cold standby (Tertiary) activation drill → verify state integrity
      - Drill result → written to Merkle Audit Trail → compliance evidence

      Agent Fleet Self-Destruction Protection (Kiro-inspired):
      - "Agent operations tier" — Skills classified by blast radius:
        Tier 0: Read-only (can't destroy anything) — all Skills default here
        Tier 1: Write to application state (can corrupt data) — requires governor approval
        Tier 2: Modify infrastructure (can take down services) — requires human approval + 2-person rule
        Tier 3: Modify Agent Fleet itself (can self-destruct) — PROHIBITED (no Skill allowed)
      - Tier 3 enforcement: any Skill attempting to modify Skill Registry / Agent Config = instant kill + audit
    implementation: "agent_fleet_bcdr.py —— MultiRegionDeployer + StateReplicationManager + FailoverOrchestrator + DisasterRecoveryDrillRunner + SelfDestructionGuard + BCDRDashboard"
    severity: high
```

### 18.2 蓝图自验证门禁与质量规则（Blueprint Self-Validation & Structural Quality Gates）

**问题陈述**：蓝图已积累 130 个盲点、62 条决策、90 项风险、62 个施工 Phase——但蓝图自身没有"完成度"的定义。当 AI 在施工前被问"这份蓝图准备好了吗？"——它只能凭感觉回答。蓝图需要的不是"更多的内容"，而是**结构完整性规则的显式定义**：每个盲点是否都有 mitigation？每个 risk 是否都有 severity？所有 Phase 是否都有文件组成声明？缺失这些，蓝图将永远处于"可以再补充一点"的无限循环中——而 13 轮审计已经证明了这一点。

ISO/IEC 42001 要求 AI 管理系统文档必须具备可审计的结构完整性。NIST AI RMF 的 Govern-Map-Measure-Manage 提供了一个证据框架——蓝图自身的完成度也应对齐这个框架。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B137:
    title: "蓝图结构自验证门禁——ISO/IEC 42001文档完整性 + NIST RMF Govern/Map/Measure/Manage对齐 + 施工准入条件"
    description: "130盲点+62决策+90风险+62Phase——但无显式定义'蓝图完成度=满足什么条件即可进入施工阶段'。蓝图审计可以无限继续——但工程需要准入条件"
    rationale: "ISO/IEC 42001: documented AI management system with structural completeness requirements; NIST AI RMF: Govern/Map/Measure/Manage as evidence framework; 13轮审计证明没有自验证门禁的蓝图会永远处于'再补一个盲点'的无限循环"
    mitigation: |
      Blueprint Self-Validation Gate (蓝图自验证门禁):

      Structural Completeness Rules:
      ```yaml
      blueprint_gates:
        G_BP_001_structural_integrity:
          description: "所有声明实体必须具有完整的结构字段"
          checks:
            - for_each(blind_spot): must_have(title, description, rationale, mitigation, implementation, severity)
            - for_each(risk): must_have(id, description, likelihood, impact, mitigation)
            - for_each(decision): must_have(id, content, date, rationale)
            - for_each(phase): must_have(name, description, file_composition, dependencies)
            - for_each(file): must_have(path, purpose, phase_association)
          auto_fix: "标记缺失字段 → 生成补充建议 → 人工审核后填入"

        G_BP_002_cross_reference_closure:
          description: "所有引用必须可解析——无悬空引用"
          checks:
            - for_each(blind_spot.references_other_blind_spots): referenced_blind_spot_must_exist
            - for_each(risk.references_blind_spot): referenced_blind_spot_must_exist
            - for_each(phase.dependencies): dependency_phase_must_exist
            - for_each(depends_on): target_module_exists
          auto_fix: "移除悬空引用 → 标记需要补充的引用实体"

        G_BP_003_nfr_consolidation:
          description: "非功能性需求必须集中声明并具有可测量SLO"
          checks:
            - must_have: latency_budget_per_operation_type
            - must_have: throughput_target_concurrent_sessions
            - must_have: availability_slo
            - must_have: token_budget_upper_bound
            - must_have: carbon_budget_daily_kgCO2  # from B134
          auto_fix: "从各章节提取分散的NFR → 汇总到统一NFR表 → 标记冲突"

        G_BP_004_glossary_completeness:
          description: "所有专业术语必须有集中定义"
          checks:
            - scan_all_sections: extract_acronyms_and_domain_terms
            - for_each(term): must_have(definition, first_used_in_section, related_terms)
          auto_fix: "自动提取术语 → AI生成定义草稿 → 人工审核批准"
      ```

      Construction Readiness Assessment (施工准入评估):
      ```yaml
      readiness_score:
        formula: "weighted_sum(gate_scores) / num_gates"
        gates:
          G_BP_001: {weight: 0.30, current_score: "TBD"}  # structural integrity
          G_BP_002: {weight: 0.25, current_score: "TBD"}  # cross-reference closure
          G_BP_003: {weight: 0.25, current_score: "TBD"}  # NFR consolidation
          G_BP_004: {weight: 0.20, current_score: "TBD"}  # glossary completeness

        thresholds:
          ready_for_scaffold: readiness_score ≥ 0.60  # 可以开始Role Skills scaffold
          ready_for_domain: readiness_score ≥ 0.75    # 可以开始Domain Skills施工
          ready_for_full: readiness_score ≥ 0.85      # 可以开始全量施工
          gold_standard: readiness_score ≥ 0.95       # 顶尖蓝图标准
      ```

      NIST RMF Alignment Check:
      - GOVERN: does blueprint define accountable roles for each Skill? ✓ (architect/implementer/governor)
      - MAP: does blueprint map Skill context, data dependencies, affected systems? ⚠ (scattered, not centralized)
      - MEASURE: does blueprint define measurable metrics per Skill? ⚠ (scattered, not centralized)
      - MANAGE: does blueprint define guardrails/kill-switch/rollback per Skill? ✓ (covered in multiple sections)

      Auto-Validation Script:
      ```python
      # blueprint_validator.py — runs on every blueprint.md change
      def validate_blueprint(blueprint_path):
          report = {
              "structural_integrity": check_all_entities_have_required_fields(),
              "cross_reference_closure": verify_no_dangling_references(),
              "nfr_consolidation": extract_and_check_nfrs(),
              "glossary_completeness": scan_for_undefined_terms(),
              "readiness_score": compute_readiness_score(),
              "recommendations": generate_fix_suggestions()
          }
          if report["readiness_score"] < 0.60:
              raise BlueprintNotReadyError(report)
          return report
      ```
    implementation: "blueprint_validator.py —— StructuralIntegrityChecker + CrossReferenceValidator + NFRExtractor + GlossaryScanner + ReadinessScoreCalculator + AutoFixSuggester"
    severity: medium
```

### 18.3 Well-Known标准化Skill发现协议（Well-Known Skill Discovery Protocol）

**问题陈述**：蓝图 §13.7 动态 Skill 发现与语义路由覆盖了"内部"Skill 发现——ZephyrAlpha 系统内各组件之间如何找到合适的 Skill。但缺失**跨组织 Skill 发现**：如果外部系统（第三方量化平台、合规审计系统、监管沙箱）需要了解 ZephyrAlpha 提供了哪些 Agent Skills——应该通过什么标准机制发现？Cloudflare 于 2026 年 1-3 月发布了 Agent Skills Discovery RFC v0.2.0——定义了 `/.well-known/agent-skills/` 作为标准化 Skill 发现端点。这一标准解决了"example.com 发布了哪些 Skills？"这一基本互操作性问题。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B138:
    title: "Well-Known标准化Skill发现——Cloudflare RFC v0.2.0 /.well-known/agent-skills/ + SHA-256完整性验证 + Archive分发"
    description: "§13.7覆盖了内部Skill发现(Embedding索引+LLM Re-ranker)——但缺失跨组织的标准化Skill发现协议。外部系统无法自动发现ZephyrAlpha的Skill目录"
    rationale: "Cloudflare Agent Skills Discovery RFC v0.2.0 (Mar 2026): /.well-known/agent-skills/ + $schema URI + SHA-256 digest per artifact + archive distribution; closes the 'what skills does example.com publish?' gap"
    mitigation: |
      Well-Known Skill Discovery:

      Discovery Endpoint:
      ```
      GET /.well-known/agent-skills/
      
      Response:
      {
        "$schema": "https://schemas.agentskills.io/discovery/0.2.0/schema.json",
        "skills": [
          {
            "name": "database-specialist",
            "description": "Database migration, SQL query, ATM two-phase commit specialist for ZephyrAlpha",
            "type": "skill-md",
            "url": "https://zephyralpha.dev/skills/domain/database/SKILL.md",
            "digest": "sha256:3a7b8c9d..."
          },
          {
            "name": "mcp-specialist",
            "description": "MCP Server creation, stdio protocol, FastMCP specialist",
            "type": "archive",
            "url": "https://zephyralpha.dev/skills/domain/mcp-server/skill.tar.gz",
            "digest": "sha256:9f8e7d6c..."
          }
        ]
      }
      ```

      Discovery Index Schema:
      - $schema: opaque identifier → clients match against known schema URIs
      - type: "skill-md" (single SKILL.md) or "archive" (tar.gz with full skill directory)
      - digest: SHA-256 of the single artifact → integrity verification before loading
      - URL: resolvable per RFC 3986

      Progressive Disclosure in Discovery:
      - L1 (Advertise ~100 tokens/skill): name + description in discovery index
      - L2 (Load < 5000 tokens): fetch SKILL.md body on demand
      - L3 (Read resources): fetch references/assets as needed
      - L4 (Run scripts): execute bundled scripts — SHALL NOT by default (security)

      Archive Distribution Safety:
      - Path traversal prevention: all paths validated against whitelist before extraction
      - Symlink rejection: symlinks in archives → REJECT (security risk)
      - Decompression bomb detection: max extracted size = 10× archive size → abort if exceeded
      - Signature verification: SHA-256 must match before extraction

      Integration with ZephyrAlpha:
      - Internal discovery (§13.7): EmbeddingIndex + LLM Reranker → for same-system routing
      - External discovery (B138): Well-Known URI → for cross-organization interoperability
      - Gateway: external requests hit Well-Known endpoint → internal requests use EmbeddingIndex
      - Audit: every external discovery request logged to Merkle Trail (B131)

      Security Considerations (Cloudflare RFC §16):
      - Well-Known endpoint is READ-ONLY — no state mutation
      - SHA-256 digest ensures skills haven't been tampered in transit
      - Clients SHOULD verify digest before loading any skill
      - Script execution from external skills: SHALL NOT by default → require explicit user opt-in
    implementation: "skill_wellknown_discovery.py —— WellKnownEndpoint + DiscoveryIndexGenerator + DigestVerifier + ArchiveValidator + CrossOrgDiscoveryAuditor"
    severity: medium
```

### 18.4 Skill数据契约与类型Schema注册表（Skill Data Contract & Type Schema Registry）

**问题陈述**：当前 Skill 间通信（§10.5 FIPA-ACL、§14.4 Concordia）依赖自然语言协商——这意味着两个 Skill 对同一个概念的语义理解可能不同。"VaR"对 risk-specialist 的含义（95% confidence parametric VaR）可能与 factor-specialist 的理解（99% confidence historical VaR）不同。Skill 通信需要 formal type schema registry——定义每种数据交换的精确结构——才能消除歧义并实现自动化验证。这一概念在 MCP Gateway Registry 的 Agent Skills Architecture 中作为 SkillCard 实体已有明确定义。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B139:
    title: "Skill间数据契约与类型Schema注册表——Formal I/O Type Contracts + Semantic Versioned Schema Registry + Per-Skill SkillCard实体"
    description: "§10.5 FIPA-ACL+§14.4 Concordia覆盖通信协议和协商——但缺失Skill间数据交换的类型契约。两个Skill可能对同一概念有不同语义理解——natural language不能替代formal type contract"
    rationale: "MCP Gateway Registry Agent Skills Architecture: SkillCard entity with formal I/O contracts + CompatibilityRequirement + ToolReference; MARIA OS: tool metadata/versioning/compatibility matrices/dependency tracking; formal type contracts eliminate semantic ambiguity in inter-skill communication"
    mitigation: |
      Skill Data Contract Registry:

      Type Schema Definition:
      ```yaml
      schema_registry:
        schemas:
          VaR_Result:
            version: "1.2.0"
            fields:
              method: {type: enum, values: [parametric, historical, monte_carlo]}
              confidence_level: {type: float, min: 0.90, max: 0.999}
              time_horizon_days: {type: int, min: 1, max: 252}
              var_value: {type: float}
              currency: {type: enum, values: [CNY, USD, EUR, HKD]}
              computation_timestamp: {type: datetime, format: ISO8601}
            validation_rules:
              - "confidence_level must be in {0.95, 0.99, 0.999} for regulatory reporting"
              - "var_value must be negative (VaR represents potential loss)"

          FactorExposure:
            version: "2.0.0"
            fields:
              factor_name: {type: string, pattern: "^[A-Z]{2,4}_FACTOR_[0-9]{3}$"}
              exposure_value: {type: float}
              standard_error: {type: float, min: 0.0}
              t_statistic: {type: float}
              r_squared: {type: float, min: 0.0, max: 1.0}
              data_period_start: {type: date}
              data_period_end: {type: date}
            validation_rules:
              - "standard_error > 0 AND standard_error < abs(exposure_value)"
              - "t_statistic > 2.0 for statistical significance"
      ```

      SkillCard Entity (per MCP Gateway Architecture):
      ```yaml
      SkillCard:
        identification:
          skill_id: "SKILL-DOM-DB-001"
          name: "database-specialist"
          path: "/skills/domain/database"
          version: "1.3.0"
        
        io_contracts:
          inputs:
            - name: "migration_request"
              schema: "MigrationRequest v1.0.0"
            - name: "query_context"
              schema: "QueryContext v1.2.0"
          outputs:
            - name: "migration_result"
              schema: "MigrationResult v1.0.0"
            - name: "query_result"
              schema: "QueryResultSet v2.1.0"
        
        compatibility:
          target_agents: ["DeepSeek-V4", "Claude-4", "GLM-5"]
          min_context_window: 32000
          requires_tools: ["Read", "Grep", "Glob", "Edit", "Write", "Bash"]
          incompatible_with: ["mcp-specialist@<2.0.0"]
        
        semantic_annotations:
          domain: "database"
          capabilities: ["migration", "query", "schema_design"]
          risk_tier: "tier_1"  # write to application state
      ```

      Schema Evolution & Compatibility:
      - Backward-compatible (PATCH): add optional field → no breaking change
      - Compatible with warning (MINOR): add required field with default → upgrade recommended
      - Breaking (MAJOR): remove/rename field or change type → all consumers must upgrade
      - Schema version policy: semver × contract version → breaking change detection at CI time

      Runtime Type Validation:
      ```python
      # Every inter-Skill message passes through SchemaValidator
      message = skill_a.create_message(
          performative="INFORM",
          content={"var_value": -0.023, "confidence_level": 0.99}
      )
      validated = schema_registry.validate(message, schema="VaR_Result v1.2.0")
      if not validated.is_valid:
          raise SchemaViolationError(validated.errors)
          # → log to audit trail → trigger auto-correction (B130)
      ```

      Schema-Aware Semantic Alignment (feeds into B124):
      - When Skill A and Skill B disagree on "VaR" → Schema Registry resolves:
        - "You sent VaR_Result v1.2.0 (parametric, 95%) but I compute VaR_Result v2.0.0 (historical, 99%)"
        - Schema adapter: auto-convert v1.2.0 ↔ v2.0.0 via defined mapping
      ```
    implementation: "skill_schema_registry.py —— TypeSchemaValidator + SchemaRegistry + SkillCardGenerator + SchemaEvolutionChecker + ContractAdapter + SchemaAwareRouter"
    severity: medium

  B140:
    title: "蓝图假设、硬约束与设计权衡显式记录——Assumptions Register + Hard Constraints Manifest + Trade-off Decision Log"
    description: "蓝图散布着隐式假设(如'AGENTS.md会被所有IDE读取'、'MCP context retrieval可用')但从未显式声明——施工时AI可能基于错误假设做灾难性决策"
    rationale: "ISO/IEC 42001: documented assumptions and constraints as AI management system requirement; NIST AI RMF Govern function: explicit risk appetite + constraints; production failures often root-caused to undocumented assumptions"
    mitigation: |
      Assumptions Register:
      ```yaml
      assumptions:
        A1_IDE_COMPATIBILITY:
          statement: "所有目标IDE (TRAE/Cursor/RooCode/Claude Code) 均读取AGENTS.md文件"
          risk_if_false: "Skill路由完全失败——系统回退到无Skill模式"
          verification: "在每个目标IDE中人工验证AGENTS.md被读取"
          owner: "implementer skill (scaffold phase)"

        A2_MCP_AVAILABILITY:
          statement: "MCP context retrieval server始终可用且延迟<200ms"
          risk_if_false: "L3 Cold Memory加载失败——Skill运行在无深度上下文模式下"
          verification: "health check endpoint + 5s probe interval (B136)"
          fallback: "使用本地文件索引作为降级方案"

        A3_MODEL_AVAILABILITY:
          statement: "至少一个推荐模型(DeepSeek/GLM/Qwen/Claude)在施工时可用"
          risk_if_false: "无法施工——Agent Spec需要LLM来执行Skill"
          verification: "model endpoint health check before any Skill execution"

        A4_NETWORK_ISOLATION:
          statement: "系统在施工时具有网络访问能力(下载依赖/查询API文档)"
          risk_if_false: "部分Skill(需要联网查询的)无法正常执行"
          fallback: "离线模式——仅使用本地缓存的依赖列表和文档"
      ```

      Hard Constraints Manifest:
      ```yaml
      hard_constraints:
        C1_ZERO_DATA_LOSS:
          description: "任何Skill执行不得导致数据丢失——所有写操作必须可回滚"
          enforcement: "pre-write checkpoint (MOD-INF-021) + WAL mode (SQLite)"

        C2_KILL_SWITCH_5S:
          description: "任何Skill终止请求必须在5秒内完成(MiFID II)"
          enforcement: "per-tool-call kill signal injection + process group SIGTERM"

        C3_NO_SKILL_SELF_MODIFICATION:
          description: "任何Skill不得修改Skill自身的定义文件或注册表"
          enforcement: "filesystem permission: skill files = read-only to agent process"

        C4_MAX_TOKEN_PER_SESSION:
          description: "单个会话的Skill总token消耗上限: 200K"
          enforcement: "token counter + MOD-INF-024 Budget Enforcer"

        C5_MIN_FRESHNESS_FOR_EXECUTION:
          description: "freshness_score < 30 的Skill拒绝执行"
          enforcement: "skill_freshness.py pre-load gate"
      ```

      Trade-off Decision Log:
      ```yaml
      tradeoffs:
        T1_COMPLETENESS_VS_SPEED:
          tradeoff: "每个模块都需要Domain Skill(完整性) vs 先施工5核心Skill再渐进扩展(速度)"
          decision: "D-019-03: scaffold先验证3 Role Skills + 5 核心 Domain Skills"
          rationale: "1人+AI维护——一次性创建19+ Skill质量无法保证"

        T2_SPECIFICITY_VS_TOKEN_BUDGET:
          tradeoff: "Domain Skill越详细(特异性↑) → token消耗越大(预算压力↑)"
          decision: "Progressive Disclosure: L1 50 tokens + L2 500 tokens + L3 on-demand"
          rationale: "Anthropic白皮书实证: frontmatter~50 + body~500最优"

        T3_DOMAIN_VS_ROLE_PRIORITY:
          tradeoff: "Domain Skill更具体(贴近领域实际) vs Role Skill更通用(跨领域一致)"
          decision: "冲突时 Domain Skill 优先(D-019-01修订)"
          rationale: "领域特异的代码模式不能被通用角色指令覆盖"

        T4_SECURITY_VS_AUTONOMY:
          tradeoff: "更多安全约束=更少自主性 vs 更少约束=更大风险"
          decision: "Autonomy Spectrum L0-L4 (D-019-12): 按Skill类型分配"
          rationale: "governor可以高自主(L3), domain-specialist中等(L2), 危险操作低自主(L0)"
      ```
    implementation: "blueprint_assumptions.py —— AssumptionsValidator + ConstraintEnforcer + TradeoffAuditor"
    severity: medium
```

### 18.5 非功能性需求汇总与术语表（Consolidated NFRs & Glossary）

**问题陈述**：蓝图散布着大量对延迟、吞吐、Token、碳足迹的约束——但它们从未集中在统一的 NFR 表中。同样，100+ 个专业术语散落在 17 个章节中——新 AI 阅读这份蓝图需要同时消化所有术语。集中术语表是一个谦逊但关键的补充——它在"让 AI 理解这份蓝图"这件事上的 ROI 可能高于任何单个新盲点的补充。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B141:
    title: "集中式非功能性需求(NFR)汇总与SLO矩阵——跨17章节的延迟/吞吐/可用性/Token/碳预算统一声明"
    description: "NFR约束散布在§2-§17各处但从未汇总——施工时无法作为统一SLO目标。Blueprint Validator(B137)的G_BP_003依赖此汇总"
    rationale: "ISO/IEC 42001: measurable performance objectives required; NIST AI RMF Measure function: metrics baselines + thresholds; consolidated SLOs enable automated SLA monitoring"
    mitigation: |
      Consolidated NFR Matrix:
      ```yaml
      nfr_matrix:
        latency:
          skill_load_time: "L1 metadata: < 10ms | L2 body: < 50ms | L3 references: < 200ms"
          skill_execution_step: "< 500ms per tool call (excl. LLM inference)"
          trigger_routing: "< 20ms (fallback: < 100ms with static routing)"
          drift_detection: "O(ms) — detect before next inference step (B130)"
          failover: "RTO < 60s (B136)"

        throughput:
          max_concurrent_skills: "3 (D-019-20: attention economics sweet spot)"
          max_concurrent_sessions: "10+ (from §1.3 运行场景约束)"
          parallel_skill_branches: "unlimited (GPU-bound, not Skill-bound)"

        availability:
          skill_registry: "99.9% (internal) — 99.99% with multi-region (B136)"
          skill_loader: "99.99% (critical path for ALL operations)"
          mcp_context_retrieval: "99.5% (L3 fallback to local file index)"
          llm_endpoint: "99.9% per model — cross-model fallback ensures overall 99.99%"

        token_budget:
          l0_always_loaded: "~800 tokens (AGENTS.md Constitution)"
          l1_metadata_per_skill: "~50 tokens each (all available Skills advertised)"
          l2_body_per_task: "Domain ~500 + Role ~300 = ≤ 800 tokens (D-019-20)"
          l3_on_demand: "≤ 8000 tokens per file"
          per_session_total: "≤ 200K tokens (Hard Constraint C4)"

        carbon_budget:  # from B134
          daily_kgco2: "≤ 5 kgCO2 (1人+AI语境, 假设50 sessions/day)"
          per_task_kgco2: "L1 edge: 0.001 | L2 mid: 0.01 | L3 large: 0.1"
          green_window_target: "≥ 40% of non-urgent tasks in green windows"

        data_quality:
          freshness_score_minimum: "≥ 30 for Skill execution (C5)"
          confidence_calibration_ece: "< 0.05 (B92: calibration drift monitor)"
          semantic_alignment_divergence: "< 5% inter-Agent (B124: core vocabulary V*)"
      ```

      SLO Dashboard Integration:
      - Grafana Panel: real-time SLO attainment per dimension
      - Error Budget tracking: 0.1% error budget for 99.9% SLO = 43min downtime/month allowed
      - Auto-alert: error budget burn rate > 10% of remaining → P1 alert

  B142:
    title: "集中式术语表与缩写定义——覆盖全部专业术语的定义、首次出现章节、关联术语索引"
    description: "100+个专业术语(ASI/FAT/KYA/FIPA-ACL/IQ/C/SCI/PRS/HuCo/GPT/BCDR等)散落在17个章节——AI阅读时没有集中术语参考。Blueprint Validator(B137)的G_BP_004依赖此术语表"
    rationale: "ISO/IEC 42001: documented terminology is foundational to AI management system; NIST AI RMF: 'harmonizing and bridging definitions for key terms'; Cloudflare RFC, agentskills.io spec all include dedicated Terminology sections"
    mitigation: |
      Glossary (术语表):
      ```yaml
      glossary:
        ASI: {full: "Agent Stability Index", definition: "12维Agent行为漂移量化指标(B126); 综合3类漂移(Semantic/Coordination/Behavioral)", first_section: "§16.3"}
        FAT: {full: "Failure Archetype Taxonomy", definition: "Kamiwaza AI 四类故障原型(FAT-1 Premature Action/FAT-2 Over-Helpfulness/FAT-3 Distractor Pollution/FAT-4 Fragile Execution)", first_section: "§16.2"}

        IQ_C: {full: "Intelligence-to-Carbon Ratio", definition: "决策质量分数/碳排放(kgCO2)的比值——用于绿色Agent调度路由", first_section: "§17.4"}

## 19. 第十四轮审计——Agent原生可观测性架构、Skill效能实证校准、自进化保真度门禁、Token经济学与Skill A/B实验框架（v0.15.0）

**审计动机**：前十三轮 138 个盲点全部从"蓝图应该有什么"出发——是一种**正向设计**视角。但多份 2026 年顶级研究论文对蓝图的核心假设提出了**实证挑战**：(1) SkillsBench（105位贡献者/7308条轨迹/11领域）证明：16/84 任务加 Skill 后性能反降，模型自己生成的 Skill 平均无收益——挑战了蓝图对 Skill 自生成/自学习的假设；(2) RAGEN（斯坦福/微软/西北大学）证明：多轮自强化会陷入"回音陷阱"——奖励方差崩溃、梯度尖峰、策略溶解而非进化——挑战了蓝图的自我改善逻辑；(3) HIT 团队（arXiv 2601.22436）证明：自进化 Agent 不可靠地消费浓缩经验——97% 忽略——挑战了知识蒸馏假设；(4) AgentTrace（AAAI 2026 LaMAS Workshop）发现 40% Agent 项目因缺乏可观测性而失败——而蓝图没有专用的 Agent 可观测性架构。本轮审计从**反向实证**视角出发——用"什么会失败"而非"什么应该做"来寻找盲点。同时关注"1人+AI维护"语境下的专属优化：故障自动分诊、渐进自动化阶梯、认知代偿策略。

**对标来源**：SkillsBench (arXiv 2602.12670, Feb 2026) + RAGEN/StarPO (Stanford/Microsoft/Northwestern, 2025/2026) + Faithful Self-Evolvers (HIT, arXiv 2601.22436, Jan 2026) + AgentTrace (AlSayyad et al., AAAI 2026 LaMAS Workshop) + MemSkill (Zhang et al., arXiv 2602, Feb 2026) + StuLife ELL Benchmark (ICLR 2026) + Tokenomics/SDLC Stage Token Mapping (Salim et al., 2026) + Elastic Agent Skills for Observability (Mar 2026) + MLflow Tracing for LLM Observability + Claude Code Wipe Production DB Incident (HN, Apr 2026) + SkillsBench Curated vs Self-Generated findings + Galileo.ai Agent Evaluation 3-Tier Rubrics。

### 19.1 Agent原生可观测性架构（Agent-Native Observability with AgentTrace 3-Surface Telemetry）

**问题陈述**：蓝图 §10.9"Real-Time Skill Observability Dashboard"仅覆盖了展示层（四面板+热力图+告警），但缺失完整的 Agent 原生可观测性架构设计。AgentTrace（AlSayyad et al., AAAI 2026 LaMAS Workshop）定义了 LLM Agent 的三类根本不同的可观察信号：**Operational**（每次方法调用+参数+返回值+耗时+状态）、**Cognitive**（LLM 提示词+补全+推理段thoughts/plans/reflections+模型标识+Token数）、**Contextual**（HTTP API/SQL/NoSQL/向量库/文件系统的对外交互——通过标准库的自动插桩补丁捕获）。三者若坍缩到单一日志流中，将**摧毁安全分析和调试所需的结构**。Elastic 于 2026 年 3 月发布的 Agent Skills for Observability 将可观测性本身封装为 Skills——证明 Agent 可观测性已经从附加功能升级为一等架构关注点。

**实证数据**（来自 2026 Q1 开发者调查）：
| 问题类型 | 占比 | 平均排查时间（无可观测性） | 有完善可观测性 |
|---------|------|--------------------------|---------------|
| LLM 返回不一致 | 34% | 4.2h | ~30min |
| Tool 调用失败 | 28% | 2.8h | ~20min |
| 上下文丢失 | 19% | 3.5h | ~25min |
| 状态管理错误 | 12% | 5.1h | ~35min |
| **排查时间倍差** | | | **6.3x** |

**真实案例**：2026年2月，某金融Agent在生产环境出现间歇性错误（错误率~3%），无完善可观测性下排查用了 **5天**——如果有AgentTrace，Traces 立即显示超时步骤、Metrics 显示 API 成功率异常、Logs 记录完整请求/响应链——预计 **30分钟**。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B143:
    title: "Agent原生可观测性架构——AgentTrace三表面Telemetry + OpenTelemetry原生集成 + eBPF内核级Trace补全"
    description: "§10.9仅覆盖Dashboard展示层——缺失Agent专属的三表面(Operational/Cognitive/Contextual)可观测性采集、结构化Envelope Schema、跨表面因果分析、eBPF补全SDK Trace丢失(实测Span完整率仅32.8%)"
    rationale: "AgentTrace (AlSayyad et al., AAAI 2026 LaMAS): 三类根本不同的信号不能坍缩为单一日志流; eBPF补全(2026奇点大会实测): SDK Span完整率32.8%→eBPF 99.1%+延迟2.4μs; Elastic Agent Skills(Mar 2026): 可观测性已封装为Skills——一等架构关注点"
    mitigation: |
      AgentTrace Three-Surface Architecture:

      Unified Envelope Schema:
      ```yaml
      TraceEnvelope:
        envelope_id: UUIDv7
        surface: [operational | cognitive | contextual]
        trace_id: UUID  # W3C TraceContext
        span_id: UUID   # parent-child hierarchy
        parent_span_id: UUID | null
        timestamp: ISO8601_UTC  # microsecond precision
        
        # Surface-specific payload
        operational_body:
          method: str
          arguments: dict
          return_value: any | null
          duration_ms: float
          status: [success | error | timeout | cancelled]
          exception: str | null
        
        cognitive_body:
          prompt: str | null
          completion: str | null
          reasoning_segments:
            - type: [thought | plan | reflection | critique]
            - content: str
            - confidence: float  # 0.0-1.0
          model: {provider, name, version}
          token_count: {input, output, reasoning}
          temperature: float
          
        contextual_body:
          interaction_type: [http | sql | nosql | vector_search | fs_io]
          target: str  # URL / table / index / path
          request: dict
          response: dict | null
          status_code: int | null
          duration_ms: float
          bytes_sent: int
          bytes_received: int
      ```

      eBPF Kernel-Level Trace Completion:
      - Problem: Agent fork子进程/WebSocket长连接/subprocess exec→绕过标准HTTP Instrumentation
      - Solution: eBPF tracepoint hook at syscall level (execve/connect/write/accept4)
      - Context propagation: trace_id injected at kernel level→survives process lifecycle
      - Measured: SDK span completeness 32.8%→eBPF 99.1%, latency overhead ~2.4μs

      Dual-Path Storage:
      - Hot Path (Real-time): Last 1h traces→Redis Streams→Grafana实时面板
      - Cold Path (Forensic): Full trace archive→Parquet/S3→Athena/Trino查询
      - Compliance Path: Tamper-evident copy→Merkle Audit Trail (B131)

      Cross-Surface Causal Analysis:
      - Cognitive "plan to query DB"→linked to Operational "execute_query()"→linked to Contextual "SELECT * FROM positions WHERE..."
      - Granularity: single trace_id chains ALL three surfaces for one logical task
      - Use case: "Why did the agent decide to sell?"→cognitive plan→operational sell()→contextual API call to broker

      Agent Observability Skills (Elastic pattern):
      - Skill "agent-observe": loaded as L3 on-demand→instruments any agent codebase
      - Auto-detects framework (LangGraph/CrewAI/MSAgent/custom)→applies correct instrumentation
      - Output: OTLP-compatible spans+native AgentTrace cognitive segments
    implementation: "agent_observability.py —— AgentTraceCollector(3 surfaces) + eBPFSpanCompletor + CrossSurfaceCorrelator + OTLPExporter + AgentObserveSkill"
    severity: high
```

### 19.2 Skill效能实证校准与抗退化门禁（SkillsBench-Inspired Efficacy Calibration & Anti-Regression Gate）

**问题陈述**：蓝图假设"添加 Skill 总能提升性能"。SkillsBench（2026年2月，105位贡献者/7308条轨迹/11领域/84任务/7模型配置）用实证数据**彻底推翻**了这一假设：

| 发现 | 数据 |
|------|------|
| Curated Skills 平均提升 | +16.2pp |
| 域间差异 | Software Engineering +4.5pp → Healthcare +51.9pp |
| **负面任务** | 16/84 tasks = **19% 性能反降** |
| Self-Generated Skills | **平均无收益（negligible or negative）** |
| Focused Skills (2-3 modules) | **优于 comprehensive documentation** |
| 小模型+Skills | **可匹配大模型无Skills** |

这意味着：**每部署5个Skill，就有1个会让系统变得更差**。蓝图缺少 Skill 效能的实证测量机制——没有配对照实验、没有抗退化门禁、没有 Skill 性能基线。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B144:
    title: "Skill效能实证校准与抗退化门禁——SkillsBench配对照实验协议 + Per-Skill效能基线 + Anti-Regression Gate（19%任务反降防护）"
    description: "蓝图假设Skill总能提升性能——SkillsBench实证：16/84任务加Skill后反降(19%)、自生成Skill零收益、2-3模块聚焦Skill优于全面文档。缺失Skill效能的配对照实验协议和抗退化门禁"
    rationale: "SkillsBench (arXiv 2602.12670, Feb 2026): 105 contributors, 7308 trajectories, 11 domains; 19% tasks regress with Skills; 3-condition design (None/Curated/Self-Generated); Focused 2-3 modules > comprehensive docs; Smaller model+Skills = Larger model-Skills"
    mitigation: |
      Skill Efficacy Paired Evaluation Protocol:

      Three-Condition Test (per SkillsBench):
      ```
      Condition A (Baseline): No Skill loaded→vanilla LLM execution
      Condition B (Curated Skill): Human-curated Skill loaded
      Condition C (Self-Generated Skill): LLM generates its own Skill
      
      For each of N test cases:
        1. Run identical task under A, B, C
        2. Measure: success_rate, latency, token_consumption, correctness_score
        3. Compute: Δ_curated = B_success - A_success
                     Δ_selfgen = C_success - A_success
        
      Efficacy Gate:
        - Δ_curated must be ≥ +3pp for Skill promotion to Canary
        - Δ_curated < 0 → Skill BLOCKED (regression detected)
        - Δ_selfgen must be ≥ +1pp for self-generated Skill to be considered
      ```

      Anti-Regression Gate:
      ```
      Gate_AR_001_skill_efficacy:
        checks:
          - for_each(new_or_modified_skill):
              paired_test_results: N ≥ 20 test cases
              min_positive_delta: Δ_curated ≥ 0 (never regress)
              min_significant_delta: Δ_curated ≥ +3pp for promotion
              domain_aware_threshold: per SkillsBench domain variance
        
        auto_actions:
          - Δ < 0: BLOCK deployment→generate regression report→notify governor
          - 0 ≤ Δ < +3pp: ALLOW Canary only→monitor closely
          - Δ ≥ +3pp: PROMOTE to full deployment
      ```

      Focused Skill Design Principle (SkillsBench-proven):
      - Anti-pattern: comprehensive 10+ module "encyclopedia" Skill
      - Optimal: 2-3 focused modules per Skill
      - Rule: if SKILL.md > 800 lines→split into 2+ focused Skills
      - Validation: auto-flag comprehensive Skills→suggest modularization

      Self-Generated Skill Reality Check:
      - SkillsBench proves: models CANNOT reliably author procedural knowledge for themselves
      - Any self-generated Skill MUST pass the same efficacy gate as curated Skills
      - Self-generated Skills that fail 3 consecutive paired evaluations→permanently retired
      - Human review required for any self-generated Skill before Canary

      Domain-Specific Efficacy Baselines:
      - Healthcare-domain Skills: expected Δ ≥ +30pp (per SkillsBench +51.9pp avg)
      - Finance-domain Skills: expected Δ ≥ +15pp (per SkillsBench ~+20pp avg)
      - Software Engineering Skills: expected Δ ≥ +3pp (per SkillsBench +4.5pp avg—lowest)
      - Skills falling below domain baseline→flagged for redesign
    implementation: "skill_efficacy_calibrator.py —— PairedEvalRunner + DeltaCalculator + AntiRegressionGate + SelfGenValidator + DomainBaselineChecker"
    severity: high

  B145:
    title: "自进化保真度门禁——RAGEN回音陷阱防护 + Faithful Self-Evolver浓缩经验验证 + EchoTrapDetector"
    description: "蓝图§15.1自纠正/§11.10自学习环假设Agent能从经验中自我改善——RAGEN证明多轮自强化导致回音陷阱(奖励方差崩溃/策略溶解)、HIT证明Agent 97%忽略浓缩经验。自进化假设需要实证修正"
    rationale: "RAGEN (Stanford/Microsoft/Northwestern): multi-turn self-reinforcement→Echo Trap(reward variance collapse+gradient spikes+strategy dissolution); HIT (arXiv 2601.22436): agents faithfully use raw experience BUT ignore 97% condensed experience; StuLife (ICLR 2026): GPT-5 scores only 17.9/100 on lifelong learning; ERL (ICLR 2026 MemAgents): single-attempt heuristics outperform multi-attempt contrastive approaches"
    mitigation: |
      Self-Evolution Fidelity Gate:

      Echo Trap Detection (RAGEN-inspired):
      ```python
      class EchoTrapDetector:
          def detect(self, evolution_history):
              signals = {
                  "reward_variance_collapse": reward_variance < threshold,
                  "gradient_spike": max_gradient > 10 * rolling_avg,
                  "policy_entropy_decay": entropy_per_step < 0.1,
                  "output_homogeneity": unique_outputs_ratio < 0.3
              }
              echo_trap_score = weighted_sum(signals)
              if echo_trap_score > 0.7:
                  # PAUSE self-evolution→inject external signal→human review
                  return EchoTrapAlert(signals)
              return Healthy
      ```

      Condensed Experience Faithfulness Check (HIT-inspired):
      - After each Skill self-improvement iteration:
        - Generate condensed heuristic (summary of what was learned)
        - Perturbation test: shuffle/corrupt the condensed heuristic
        - If agent behavior DOES NOT CHANGE→experience is NOT faithfully used
        - If unfaithful for 3 consecutive iterations→disable self-evolution for this Skill
      
      Human-in-the-Loop Correction (1人+AI语境):
      - Self-evolution produces "improvement proposal" (NOT automatic application)
      - Human reviews proposal every N iterations (N = 5 for stable Skills, N = 3 for new)
      - Human approves/rejects/modifies→approved changes applied
      - Rejected proposals logged with reason→feeds back to evolution algorithm
      
      Safe Self-Evolution Sandbox:
      - Self-evolution runs in ISOLATED environment (not on production Skill)
      - Produces "candidate skill v_next" in sandbox
      - Candidate passes: efficacy gate (B144) + echo trap check + faithfulness check
      - Only then promoted to Canary→Production

      MemSkill-Compatible Design:
      - Memory operations as learnable, evolvable Skills (not fixed rules)
      - Designer (human) periodically inspects memory-Skill failures
      - Updates memory-Skill set: refine existing, invent new
      - Memory toolkit evolves to match real usage, not developer assumptions
      
      Collapse Recovery Protocol:
      - When Echo Trap detected: revert to last known-good checkpoint
      - Inject diversity signal: random exploration noise for next K iterations
      - Human reviews collapse pattern→adjust evolution hyperparameters
      - After 3 successful non-collapse iterations→re-enable self-evolution
    implementation: "self_evolution_fidelity_gate.py —— EchoTrapDetector + FaithfulnessChecker + HumanApprovalPipeline + SandboxEvolutionRunner + CollapseRecoveryProtocol"
    severity: high
```

### 19.3 Skill Token经济学与成本归因（Skill Tokenomics — Stage-Level Cost Attribution）

**问题陈述**：蓝图 §11.4 定义了注意力经济学和 Token 预算上限（200K/会话、3 Skill 活跃上限），但从未对 Token 消耗做**阶段性归因分析**。Salim et al.（2026）的 Tokenomics 研究发现：Code Review 阶段消耗 59.4% 的 Token（而非直觉认为的 Code Generation 占大头），Input Token 占平均 53.9%（"通信税"——Agent 间每轮传递完整累积上下文），Documentation 阶段 Input Token 占 80.2%。没有阶段级 Token 归因，优化就是盲飞——你不知道 Token 烧在哪里，就无法优化哪里。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B146:
    title: "Skill Token经济学——SDLC六阶段Token归因 + 输入Token'通信税'优化 + Per-Stage Cost Dashboard"
    description: "蓝图有Token预算但无阶段级归因——Salim et al.(2026): Code Review消耗59.4% Token(非Code Gen)、Input Token占53.9%('通信税')。不归因就无法优化——优化Token预算的第一步是知道Token烧在哪里"
    rationale: "Tokenomics/SDLC Stage Token Mapping (Salim et al., 2026): 30 tasks on ChatDev with GPT-5; Code Review=59.4%/Design=2.4%/Coding=6.9%; Input tokens dominate 53.9% avg across all stages; Doc=80.2% input tokens; 1-person context: $30-150/day AI cost"
    mitigation: |
      SDLC Stage Token Attribution:

      Token Stage Mapping:
      ```yaml
      sdlc_stages:
        Design: {token_share: ~2.4%, input_ratio: ~40%}
        Coding: {token_share: ~6.9%, input_ratio: ~12%}
        Code_Completion: {token_share: ~18.5%, input_ratio: ~65%}
        Code_Review: {token_share: ~59.4%, input_ratio: ~70%}  # DOMINANT
        Testing: {token_share: ~10.2%, input_ratio: ~55%}
        Documentation: {token_share: ~2.6%, input_ratio: ~80.2%}
      ```

      Communication Tax Reduction Strategies:
      - Per-Agent context deduplication: remove redundant entries from passed context
      - Delta-only context passing: pass only Δ since last interaction (not full history)
      - Progressive context loading: Agent loads only relevant portion, not full chain
      - Target: reduce input token ratio from 53.9%→<35%
      
      Code Review Token Optimization (targeting the 59.4%):
      - L1 automated review (Haiku/cheap model) for pattern matching→catches 60% issues
      - L2 deep review (Sonnet/Opus) only for L1-flagged items→reduces expensive review by 70%
      - Review context pruning: only send changed code + immediate dependencies (not full repo)
      - Estimated savings: 59.4%→~20% token share in Code Review
      
      Per-Skill Token Ledger:
      ```python
      class SkillTokenLedger:
          def record(self, skill_id, stage, input_tokens, output_tokens):
              self.ledger[skill_id][stage] += {
                  "input": input_tokens, 
                  "output": output_tokens,
                  "cost": compute_cost(input_tokens, output_tokens)
              }
          
          def anomaly_detect(self):
              # Flag: any stage consuming >2σ above rolling average
              # Alert: total daily token cost exceeds budget by >20%
              pass
      ```

      1人+AI Cost Controls (DevLLMOps-inspired):
      - Set hard daily token budget: $50/day (1-person context)
      - Model tiering: Haiku for pattern-match tasks, Sonnet for reasoning, Opus for architecture
      - Use .agentignore to exclude irrelevant files from agent context
      - Decompose large intents into smaller focused tasks (less context per run)
      - Weekly cost trend report→auto-flag upward trajectory >15%
    implementation: "skill_tokenomics.py —— SDLCStageMapper + CommunicationTaxReducer + CodeReviewOptimizer + TokenLedger + CostAnomalyDetector"
    severity: medium
```

### 19.4 Skill A/B实验框架与端到端验证场景（Skill A/B Testing & End-to-End Walkthroughs）

**问题陈述**：蓝图 §11.8 影子部署和 §11.12 Feature Flag 覆盖了渐进发布——但缺少**正式的 A/B 实验框架**来科学地判定"Skill v1.1 > v1.0"。同时，蓝图是 capability-deep 但 scenario-shallow——零个 ZephyrAlpha 真实端到端场景。对于100% AI 施工来说，没有具体场景，实施 AI 将对 Skill 交互模式产生幻觉。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B147:
    title: "Skill A/B实验框架——Treatment/Control分流 + 统计功效计算 + Per-Metric显著性判定 + Experiment Lifecycle Management"
    description: "§11.8影子部署+§11.12 FeatureFlag覆盖渐进发布——但缺失科学A/B实验框架：如何判定Skill v1.1确实优于v1.0？需要分流/功效/显著性/实验生命周期"
    rationale: "Galileo.ai Agent Evaluation (Feb 2026): 3-tier rubrics + trajectory vs outcome metrics + CI/CD integration; SkillsBench: paired eval作为Skill评估的核心范式; 40% agentic AI projects canceled by 2027 (Gartner)"
    mitigation: |
      Skill A/B Experimentation Framework:

      Experiment Lifecycle:
      ```
      1. HYPOTHESIS: "Skill v1.1 reduces latency by 15% without accuracy loss"
      2. POWER ANALYSIS: compute required sample size
         - α = 0.05, β = 0.20 (80% power)
         - MDE (Minimum Detectable Effect) = 10% improvement
         - → N = max(100, computed_sample_size) per arm
      3. RANDOMIZATION: hash(user_id + task_id) % 100→treatment/control
      4. RUN: 50:50 split, auto-stop when N reached or significance achieved
      5. ANALYZE: per-metric p-value + effect size + CI
      6. DECIDE: promote / iterate / discard
      ```

      Multi-Metric Evaluation:
      ```yaml
      primary_metrics:
        - task_success_rate: "Did Skill complete the task correctly?"
        - time_to_completion: "How fast?"
      
      guardrail_metrics (must NOT degrade):
        - error_rate: "Did errors increase?"
        - token_consumption: "Did cost increase >10%?"
        - user_satisfaction: "Did human rating drop?"
      
      secondary_metrics:
        - reasoning_quality: rubric score (B144)
        - recovery_rate: self-healing success (B145)
        - hallucination_rate: HuCo detection rate (B130)
      ```

      Significance Decision Matrix:
      ```
      | Primary Δ | Guardrail Δ | Decision |
      |-----------|-------------|----------|
      | Sig +     | All OK      | PROMOTE  |
      | Sig +     | 1 degraded  | REVIEW→human decides |
      | Not sig   | All OK      | ITERATE→larger sample |
      | Sig -     | Any         | DISCARD  |
      ```

      Experiment Registry & Governance:
      - All experiments logged: {id, hypothesis, dates, results, decision}
      - Maximum concurrent experiments: 3 (attention economics, B126)
      - Experiment auto-terminate: 7 days or N reached, whichever first
      - P-hacking prevention: pre-registered metrics→no post-hoc metric shopping
    implementation: "skill_ab_test.py —— HypothesisRegistry + PowerAnalyzer + TreatmentSplitter + MultiMetricEvaluator + DecisionEngine + ExperimentGovernor"
    severity: medium

  B148:
    title: "ZephyrAlpha端到端Skill交互场景Walkthrough——最少3条完整触发→执行→反馈链路 + Per-Scenario Skill Fire Order + Failure Mode演练"
    description: "蓝图capability-deep但scenario-shallow——零个ZephyrAlpha真实场景端到端演练。100% AI施工+1人维护：无具体场景→实施AI将幻觉Skill交互模式"
    rationale: "1人+AI施工：场景是实施AI理解'Skills如何协作'的唯一锚点; 蓝图138个盲点定义了能力但未展示它们在真实ZephyrAlpha工作流中如何串联; 缺失场景=施工AI将在真空中编排Skills"
    mitigation: |
      End-to-End Walkthrough Scenarios:

      Scenario 1: "New Database Migration"
      ```
      TRIGGER: developer asks "migrate positions table to ATM two-phase commit pattern"
      
      Skill Fire Order:
      T+0ms: AGENTS.md (L0 always loaded) → identifies trigger pattern
      T+10ms: Trigger Table (§2.2) → matches "database"+"ATM" → scores:
              - database-specialist [domain]: 0.89 ← HIGHEST
              - implementer [role]: 0.62
              - architect [role]: 0.45
      T+20ms: implementer [role] loads L2 body (300 tokens)
              → reads blueprint §3.3 ATM patterns
              → delegates to database-specialist [domain]: "apply ATM two-phase commit to positions"
      T+30ms: database-specialist [domain] loads L2 body (500 tokens)
              → scans existing schema→proposes migration DDL
      T+50ms: gate-specialist [domain] (auto-triggered per B92 calibration gate)
              → validates migration SQL syntax+idempotency key
      T+80ms: output guardrail (§13.5) → Rule layer: "DDL must include BEGIN/COMMIT"
              → NL layer: confirms ATM pattern applied correctly
              → Passes→returns to user
      
      Failure Mode Walkthrough — Scenario 1a:
      - database-specialist proposes wrong isolation level (READ COMMITTED vs SERIALIZABLE)
      - gate-specialist detects freshness_score < 30→declines validation
      - governor invokes drift-detector (§16.3)→flags semantic drift: "SERIALIZABLE required for ATM"
      - database-specialist self-corrects (§15.1, T1 re-route)
      - Recovery Audit Trail written (§15.1) + Merkle Audit Tree updated (B131)
      ```

      Scenario 2: "Portfolio VaR Computation"
      ```
      TRIGGER: analyst asks "compute 1-day 99% VaR for portfolio P7"
      
      Skill Fire Order:
      T+0ms: AGENTS.md + Trigger Table
              → risk-specialist [domain]: 0.91
              → factor-specialist [domain]: 0.42
              → market-specialist [domain]: 0.38
      T+10ms: risk-specialist loads→queries Schema Registry (B139) for VaR_Result v1.2.0
      T+20ms: risk-specialist needs portfolio composition→calls data-access-specialist
              FIPA-ACL: REQUEST {performative: REQUEST, content: "portfolio P7 holdings"}
      T+40ms: data-access-specialist responds INFORM {holdings_json}
      T+50ms: risk-specialist needs market data→calls market-data-specialist
              FIPA-ACL: REQUEST {performative: REQUEST, content: "daily returns 252 days"}
      T+80ms: market-data-specialist→concordia calendar check (B131 temporal awareness)
              → confirms within market hours→fetches data→INFORM
      T+100ms: risk-specialist computes VaR (parametric, 99%, 1-day)
               → validates against Schema VaR_Result v1.2.0
               → HuCo hallucination check (B130): passes
               → confidence calibration (B92): ECE < 0.05→score 0.93
      T+120ms: output returns to user: "VaR(99%, 1d) = -2.34%, CVaR = -3.17%"
      ```

      Scenario 3: "ADR Decision Record Creation"
      ```
      TRIGGER: after completing feature, developer asks "record this as ADR"
      
      Skill Fire Order:
      T+0ms: Trigger Table→architect [role]: 0.78→governor [role]: 0.45
      T+10ms: architect loads→reads feature diff→extracts: {context, decision, consequences}
      T+20ms: architect queries KB for ADR template (B139 Schema: ADR_Record v1.0.0)
      T+30ms: architect writes decision to KB decisions namespace→proposes commit message
      T+40ms: gate-specialist auto-triggered→validates ADR against Schema
              → checks: {status: [proposed|accepted|deprecated|superseded],
                         context, decision, consequences} all present
      T+50ms: knowledge-specialist cross-references with existing ADRs
              → no conflicts detected→marks ADR as "accepted"
      T+60ms: Merkle Audit Trail (B131): ADR creation logged with:
              - Who: architect skill v1.3.5
              - What: created ADR-042
              - When: 2026-05-05T14:32:01.234567Z
              - Evidence: diff link + KB cross-reference results
      ```
    implementation: "skill_scenario_walkthrough.py —— ScenarioRunner + SkillFireOrderTracer + FailureModeSimulator + WalkthroughDashboard"
    severity: medium

### 19.5 1人+AI维护专属优化（Solo Maintenance — Triage Tiers + Automation Escalation Ladder + Cognitive Compensation）

**问题陈述**：蓝图 §12.7"1人+AI维护认知退化防护"覆盖了技能保持（AI Dependency Index / Weekly Manual Sprint / Atrophy Risk Alert）。但缺少1人维护中最关键的**运营分流体系**：每天生产环境中会产生大量 Skill 告警/异常/冲突——哪些需要人看、哪些 AI 自己处理、处理的自动化程度如何逐步提升？没有分级分流，"1人"会被告警淹没。

Claude Code Wipe Production DB Incident（HN 2026年4月）：Agent 执行了 Terraform 命令删除了生产数据库——事后讨论的共识是"如果给机器人删除生产的能力，它就会删除生产"。1人维护场景下，风险信号需要在 Agent 造成不可逆破坏前被拦截。

```yaml
blind_spots:
  B149:
    title: "1人+AI维护专属——运营分流五级Triage + 渐进自动化阶梯(L0→L4) + Solo Dev认知代偿策略 + Agent破坏半径约束"
    description: "§12.7覆盖技能保持——但缺失1人维护的核心：每天大量告警/异常/冲突→哪些需要人看、哪些AI自处理、自动化如何渐进提升？无分流=1人被告警淹没"
    rationale: "Claude Code Wipe Prod DB (HN Apr 2026): Agent删除了生产数据库→'给机器人能力，它就会用'; 1人维护不可承受Agent造成的不可逆破坏; DevLLMOps: 无自然成本上限的问题+$30-150/天; 90% AI gains→需求分流体系"
    mitigation: |
      Alert & Anomaly Triage System:

      Five-Tier Triage:
      ```
      T0 — AUTO-RESOLVE (no human needed, < 1%):
        - Cache miss→auto-refill from warm pool
        - Single-tool transient timeout→auto-retry (≤3 attempts)
        - Minor freshness degradation (score 27-29)→auto-schedule refresh
        - Action: auto-resolve, log to daily digest only
        
      T1 — AUTO-HEAL (self-healing attempt, no human):
        - Skill output schema validation failure→auto-rollback+retry
        - Single Skill circuit breaker OPEN→wait+auto-retry (B126)
        - Drift detected < 2σ→auto-recalibrate (B130)
        - Action: auto-heal, log recovery action, success/fail→T2 if fails 3x
        
      T2 — AI-TRIAGE (AI analyzes, proposes fix, human approves daily batch):
        - Multi-Skill consensus failure (3+ Skills disagree)→AI generates reconciliation
        - Knowledge decay alert (freshness < 20)→AI proposes re-learning plan
        - Minor adversarial pattern detected→AI suggests countermeasure
        - Action: AI drafts resolution, batched for human review (daily digest)
        
      T3 — HUMAN-REQUIRED (human must decide, < 4h SLA):
        - Skill efficacy regression detected (Δ < 0, B144)→human reviews experimental data
        - Echo Trap detected (B145)→human decides: revert or adjust
        - Compliance boundary approached (e.g., near kill-switch trigger)→human authorizes
        - Action: push notification, human must respond < 4h
        
      T4 — EMERGENCY (human MUST intervene immediately, < 5min SLA):
        - Agent attempting Tier 2+ operation (modify infrastructure, B136)
        - Production database write detected from non-approved Skill
        - Kill-switch boundary crossed (MiFID II Art 17.1)
        - Agent self-modification attempt detected (Hard Constraint C3 violation)
        - Action: auto-kill offending Skill + urgent notification (SMS/call)
      ```

      Progressive Automation Ladder:
      ```
      Phase 0 (Week 1-2): ALL actions require human approval (T0-T4)
        → Human builds trust in Skill decision quality
        
      Phase 1 (Week 3-4): T0 auto-resolve enabled
        → Human reviews daily digest→approves automation quality
        
      Phase 2 (Month 2): T0 + T1 auto-heal enabled
        → Weekly review of auto-heal decisions
        → Adjust thresholds based on false-positive rate
        
      Phase 3 (Month 3-4): T0 + T1 + AI-drafted T2 proposals
        → Human reviews T2 batch daily→approves/rejects each
        → Track acceptance rate→when >90%→promote to Phase 4
        
      Phase 4 (Month 5+): T0 + T1 auto, T2 AI-proposed with 24h window
        → Human reviews weekly instead of daily
        → Only T3+T4 require immediate human attention
        
      Phase 5 (Steady State): Human handles T3+T4 only (~5% of total alerts)
        → Monthly full review of T0-T2 automated decisions
        → Calibration adjustment if false-positive rate > 2%
      ```

      Agent Destruction Radius Constraint (Kiro + Claude Wipe Prod DB):
      ```
      Tier 0: Read-only Skills→no blast radius
        - All Skills default to Tier 0
      
      Tier 1: Write to application state→blast radius ≤ 1 module
        - Requires governor approval per operation
        - Auto-rollback checkpoint before every write (MOD-INF-021)
      
      Tier 2: Modify infrastructure→blast radius ≤ 1 service
        - Requires HUMAN approval + governor co-sign
        - Dry-run mandatory before execution
        - Cannot run between 22:00-06:00 (night safety window)
      
      Tier 3: Modify Agent Fleet itself→PROHIBITED
        - Any attempt→auto-kill + T4 emergency alert
        - No Skill has Tier 3 permissions—enforced at OS level
      ```

      Solo Dev Cognitive Compensation:
      - "Decision Journal" auto-generated: every human decision logged with context
      - "Skill Trust Dashboard": per-Skill acceptance rate→guides human attention allocation
      - "Weekly Autonomy Report": % of alerts handled automatically→trend toward Phase 5
      - "Bus Factor Mitigation": all Skill configuration + operational knowledge in AGENTS.md
    implementation: "solo_maintenance_triage.py —— FiveTierClassifier + AutoEscalationLadder + HumanApprovalPipeline + DestructionRadiusEnforcer + DecisionJournal + AutonomyReporter"
    severity: medium
```
```
